===G:/demo/21_xiaozhi/code-server-mirror/manager-api/src/main/java/xiaozhi/common/exception/===

RenException.java[CG7T]: F:自定义运行时异常，携带错误码并经MessageUtils按code解析国际化消息 | R:code:manager-api/src/main/java/xiaozhi/common/utils/MessageUtils.java,code:manager-api/src/main/java/xiaozhi/common/exception/ErrorCode.java | A:构造器(code[,params][,cause])与(String msg[,cause])重载、getCode/getMsg | S:msg与super.getMessage()重复存储需用getMsg取值；String构造默认INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR；code必须命中i18n资源key否则解析失败
RenExceptionHandler.java[CR8T]: F:全局REST异常拦截器，将自定义、校验、唯一键冲突、Shiro权限及兜底异常统一转为Result错误响应 | R:code:manager-api/src/main/java/xiaozhi/common/exception/RenException.java,code:manager-api/src/main/java/xiaozhi/common/utils/Result.java,code:manager-api/src/main/java/xiaozhi/common/exception/ErrorCode.java | A:HTTP错误响应统一为Result JSON格式(code/msg) | S:Shiro UnauthorizedException映射为FORBIDDEN(403)而非401；参数校验仅取首条非空错误消息；兜底Exception写日志但不向前端泄露堆栈；404与校验消息经MessageUtils做i18n解析

JsonRpcTwo.java[CG4T]: F:封装JSON-RPC 2.0请求载体，默认版本号并承载method/params/id | R:- | A:- | S:id限定Integer不支持字符串id；仅建模请求无error成员；jsonrpc字段固定默认"2.0"
JsonUtils.java[CG8S]: F:封装Jackson单例ObjectMapper提供JSON序列化、反序列化及Map/List类型转换工具 | R:- | A:导出静态API toJsonString/parseObject/parseArray/parseMap/toStringObjectMap等 | S:全局共享ObjectMapper未做任何定制配置；解析异常统一包装为RuntimeException而非返回空值；空字符串按方法返回null或空ArrayList语义不一致
